That, and the men whispering in the walls... that's what finally scared my wife enough to make this appointment. I don't... I haven't noticed anything else.\n\nDoctor: Do you hear the whispering or see the dark wet patch only at home, or can it happen anywhere?\n\nPatient: I... I don't really know. I don't go out much. \n\nSo... just at home, I guess. I haven't really noticed it anywhere else.\n\nDoctor: Are you able to perform your daily activities normally despite these experiences?\n\nPatient: No. Not really. \n\nI... I don't do much of anything anymore. I haven't been keeping up with things around the house... repairs, paying the bills... my wife has to do all of that now. Make all the decisions. \n\nIt... it got pretty bad recently. She actually had to call some kind of crisis team. I just... I couldn't handle it when they needed to do a safety inspection here. I couldn't cooperate with them. So... no, things aren't normal.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling sad or down most of the time lately?\n\nPatient: Yeah... I guess so. Most days, I wake up and I just... I already feel like I've failed. Like I'm failing as a husband. As a man. It's just this... heaviness. Just... just the whispering. And the patch on the floor. Nothing else.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe the men whispering in the walls are actually trying to communicate with you or are plotting something against you?\n\nPatient: I... I don't know about plotting. They're just there. But... it's not just them. \n\nI know how it sounds. But I'm... I'm sure about the house. The old house. Just waiting for me to make another mistake. Waiting for me to ruin things again.\n\nDoctor: Do you really believe the neighbors are watching you and waiting for you to make a mistake?\n\nPatient: I just... I can tell. They are. Make all the decisions. \n\nIt... it got pretty bad recently. She actually had to call some kind of crisis team. I just... I couldn't handle it when they needed to do a safety inspection here. I couldn't cooperate with them. So... no, things aren't normal.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling sad or down most of the time lately?\n\nPatient: Yeah... I guess so. Most days, I wake up and I just... I already feel like I've failed. Like I'm failing as a husband. As a man. It's just this... heaviness. Just waiting for me to make another mistake. Nothing else.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe the men whispering in the walls are actually trying to communicate with you or are plotting something against you?\n\nPatient: I... I don't know about plotting. They're just there. But... it's not just them. \n\nI know how it sounds. But I'm... I'm sure about the house. The old house. The mold in it... the bad wiring... it got into everything. It poisoned all my things. My clothes, my papers. And... \n\n...and the neighbors. They know. I can feel them watching me. Just waiting for me to make another mistake. Waiting for me to ruin things again.\n\nDoctor: Do you really believe the neighbors are watching you and waiting for you to make a mistake?\n\nPatient: I just... I can tell. They are. They're watching. I know it probably sounds... far-fetched to you. But I'm certain about it. Just like I'm certain the mold and the wiring from the old house poisoned everything I own. They just... they know. And they're waiting.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the neighbors' supposed watching is related to the whispering you hear in the walls or the dark patch on the floor?\n\nPatient: I don't know if they're connected. I just... I know none of this was happening before. \n\nBefore the house problems dragged on and I fell into this... this depression, I never heard voices in the walls. Or saw wet patches that weren't there. I didn't think the neighbors were tracking me back then. All of that... it only showed up after I had already stopped caring about my usual life. After I started feeling like a failure every day. So... I don't know.\n\nDoctor: Do you really think the mold and wiring from the old house actually poisoned your belongings?\n\nPatient: Yes. I... I know it sounds far-fetched. But I'm certain the mold and wiring in that house poisoned everything I own. And... I can tell the neighbors are watching to see when I mess up again. I don't really know how else to explain it to you.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the belief that the mold and wiring poisoned your belongings is something that might not be true, or are you completely certain of it?\n\nPatient: I... I thought I just said. I am sure. \n\nI know it sounds far-fetched to you, but... yes. I'm completely certain the mold and the wiring in that house poisoned everything I own. And... I can tell the neighbors are watching to see when I mess up again. I'm just certain.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible that your beliefs about the mold, wiring, and neighbors might not be based in reality?\n\nPatient: I don't know why you keep asking me the same thing. \n\nNo. It's real. I already told you I'm certain. My things are ruined from that house... the poison is there. And those people next door are just waiting for me to slip up. I'm not making it up. I don't really want to keep repeating myself.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there's any possibility that your belief about the neighbors watching you is not true?\n\nPatient: No. I... I told you already. I know they're watching. I can just tell. I don't see why we have to keep going over this.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the neighbors are watching you because they want to harm you?\n\nPatient: I don't know about harm. I just... I told you, I know they're watching to see when I mess up again. Like I said, the mold and the wiring poisoned my things, and they're just... waiting for me to fail. I don't really want to talk about them anymore.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible that the whispering in the walls is just a product of your own mind?\n\nPatient: No... it's not my mind. I hear them. \n\nIt's... it's what finally scared my wife, actually. I keep hearing the men whispering in there, and... and water running, even when nobody else is around. And sometimes... I see this dark, wet patch spreading across the hallway. But when I check it, it's dry. \n\nBut I know what I hear. It's not in my head.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the whispering in the walls is talking about you or something related to your life?\n\nPatient: I don't... I can't really make out the exact words. \n\nLike I just said, it's just... men whispering in there. That, and the water running. I don't know if it's about me. It's just there. It's what scared my wife so much, me hearing it and... and checking that dark wet spot in the hall over and over when it's actually dry. \n\nI don't... I don't really want to guess what they're saying.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief about the mold and wiring poisoning your belongings could be wrong?\n\nPatient: No. I know it sounds... far-fetched to you. But I'm certain. \n\nThe mold... the wiring... it poisoned everything I own. I just know it did. And... like I said before, I can tell the neighbors are watching. Just waiting for me to mess up again. I don't think I'm wrong about any of it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the neighbors are watching you because they want to cause you harm?\n\nPatient: I don't know if they want to hurt me. Like I just said... I can just tell they're watching to see when I mess up again. \n\nI know the whole thing sounds far-fetched to you... about the mold and the wiring poisoning everything I own. But I'm certain of it. And they just... they just watch. I don't really want to talk about them anymore.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible that your beliefs about the mold, wiring, and neighbors could change if you received evidence that proved they were not true?\n\nPatient: No. I wouldn't believe it. \n\nI know... I know it sounds far-fetched. You've made that clear. But I'm certain. The mold, the wiring... it poisoned everything I own. Evidence isn't going to change what I know is true. And I can just tell the neighbors are watching... waiting for me to mess up. \n\nI'm just... I'm certain. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your beliefs about the mold, wiring, and neighbors might be considered delusions by others?\n\nPatient: I don't know what other people think. They probably do think I'm crazy. But... like I keep saying, I know it sounds far-fetched. I'm just certain the mold and the wiring poisoned everything. And the neighbors... they are watching. I don't really care what someone else calls it. It's just what's happening."
